# Grecharged-secondary-motion — data-driven chain params (hot-editable; adb push + toggle physics to reload)
# classes: primary=1 secondary=2 accessory=4 ; stiffness in Hz ; maxangle in degrees ; radius in game units (4096/m)
#
# CHAIN KEYS
#   stiffness damping gravity maxangle inertia stretch radius   (as before)
#   rootlock=N   the first N links ride the animated bone RIGIDLY — no integration, no cone, no
#                write-back. This is what keeps a hair chain's ROOT welded to the skull instead of
#                the whole strand floating free (owner cycle-2 defect B). Clamped to len-1, so a
#                single-joint chain is never fully frozen.
#   gradient=G   0 = uniform (pre-cycle-2), 1 = full root->tip freedom ramp: the first free link
#                keeps 1/nfree of the swing cone and is proportionally stiffer, the tip keeps the
#                full authored amplitude. Only meaningful with >=2 free links.
#   anim=MODE    what to do with the AUTHORED animation channel on a physics-owned joint. jak1
#                already hand-keys pseudo-wind on straps/hair/cloth, and springing toward that pose
#                left two motions fighting (owner cycle-2 defect A: Jak's blue cloth clipping —
#                shirtLthigh/shirtRthigh were replaying the driver's ENTIRE leg swing).
#                  replace (DEFAULT) — the joint is re-glued to its bind-pose local carried by the
#                    animated parent; the authored channel is dropped and physics owns the joint.
#                  excite  — same re-glue, but the dropped channel comes back as an input FORCE
#                    scaled by excite= (never a second writer). Use where the channel is ACTING
#                    rather than fake physics: Daxter's ears/tail, Samos' beard.
#                  keep    — pre-cycle-2 behaviour (spring toward the authored pose).
#   excite=X     force scale for anim=excite. 1.0 reproduces `keep` exactly, so the mode is monotone.
#   couple=K     ANCHOR COUPLING GAIN (cycle 7). Until this key the ONLY thing that excited a chain
#                was the lag it develops behind its own moving target, and for a damped spring that
#                lag is a/omega^2 — so amplitude was tied to 1/stiffness^2 and the owner's "plus de
#                jiggle ET un poil plus de fermete" was arithmetically impossible: every notch of
#                firmness was paid for out of the travel. couple= adds the pseudo-force of the
#                accelerating ANCHOR FRAME (the anchor's world position, differenced twice) as an
#                explicit input, so the chain obeys
#                    d'' + 2*zeta*w*d' + w^2*d = -(1 + couple) * a_anchor
#                and AMPLITUDE (couple) is finally independent of FIRMNESS (stiffness). 0 = OFF,
#                which is every chain that does not name it, so adding the key moved nothing that
#                was already accepted. Clamped to 8 in GOAL; the second difference itself is bounded
#                by PHYS-COUPLE-MAX * chainlen so an animation POP cannot become a kick.
#   friction=F   0..1 contact friction: the collision push-out is absorbed into the verlet `prev`
#                instead of reading as free velocity next substep (kills contact buzz). Default 0.5.
#
# CYCLE-3 CHAIN KEYS (owner verdict 2026-08-06)
#   authored=T   AUTHORED ANIMATION HAS PRIORITY.
#                MEASURED LIMIT, READ THIS BEFORE ARMING A NEW CHAIN: T compares the authored pose
#                to the calm rest pose, so it also sees a STATIC pose offset — a character whose
#                idle simply holds a bone away from its bind pose reads as "the animator is driving
#                it" forever, and full suspension then deletes that chain's physics silently. Armed
#                cast-wide on every anim=excite chain, the x86 smoke measured exactly that:
#                Daxter's ears/tail sit at 0.53-1.36 of chain length CONTINUOUSLY and Samos' stock
#                beard at a flat 0.425 (holdmax 2697 frames = 45 s of unbroken suspension). So it is
#                armed ONLY where the owner named a discrete forced action, with a threshold above
#                that chain's MEASURED routine level. To arm another chain: read its `aratio:` in
#                the [HD-PHYS2] window line first, then pick a threshold above its routine peak —
#                the holdmax gate in physics_x86_smoke.sh fails the build if you get it wrong. T is the authored offset, as a fraction of the
#                chain's own length, at which ND is judged to be DRIVING these bones on purpose
#                (Jak's ears reacting, Keira GRABBING her goggles). Above T the sim hands the chain
#                over: the target becomes the authored pose, gravity/excitation fade out, collisions
#                stop. Below T/2 it takes it back, progressively. 0 = OFF, and 0 IS THE DEFAULT —
#                jak1's hand-keyed pseudo-wind would also cross a global threshold, and the owner's
#                cycle-2 verdict is that the wind stays removed. Opt in only where ND ACTS.
#   authrise=R   blend-in rate per second (how fast the animation takes over).
#   authfall=F   blend-out rate per second — this is the "physics resumes progressively" ramp.
#   authstiff=S  stiffness multiplier at full authored weight (how tightly the authored pose tracks).
#   rootfree=X   FLOOR of the root->tip freedom ramp, 0..1. Default 0.3. The raw ramp gives the
#                stiffest free link 1/nfree of the cone, which is right on an 8-link cape and
#                useless on a 2-link one — that is why Jak's coiffe and Keira's short back hair
#                both read as "only the very tip moves". Can only RAISE freedom, never lower it.
#   colskip=N    the first N links are INSIDE the body on purpose (a hair root is glued to the
#                skull) — exempt from collision push-out AND from the residual audit. Until cycle 3
#                this was an accident of rootlock=; freeing a root to MOVE without it would weld
#                every hair root against its own skull. Effective value is max(rootlock, colskip).
#   nodesc=1     do NOT rigid-re-glue this chain's descendants (they carry their own animation).
#
# CYCLE-3 COLLIDER KEY
#   radius2=R    FAR-END radius: the volume becomes a CONE instead of a cylinder, and the contact
#                radius is interpolated at the contact point. A constant radius cannot model a
#                FLARED trouser leg or a shoulder, which is why Jak's jacket hem clipped through a
#                capsule that was correctly sized for his thigh (owner cycle-3 C).
#
# TWO OWNER-LISTED SITES HAVE NO BONE TO DRIVE — documented, not dropped (see the phase report):
#   * Jak's metal RING on his chest-plate (owner cycle-3 E) and the metal BUCKLE on the strap in his
#     back (owner cycle-3 H). Neither jak-hd (75 joints) nor eichar-lod0 (83) has any ring/buckle/
#     plate/medal/pendant/clasp joint, and a scan of ALL 458 shipped rigs in
#     decompiler/config/jak1/ntsc_v1/joint-node-info.min.json returns zero hits game-wide. Both are
#     rigidly skinned to chest / packStrapMid. Giving them physics needs a bone INJECTED at HD prep
#     plus a weight transfer on the mesh — an asset-pipeline job, not a line in this file.
#
# COLLIDER KEYS  (owner 17:35: a REAL body volume, not 1-2 symbolic spheres)
#   collider <joint> radius=R [chains=a,b] [tier=N]          sphere on one animated bone
#   capsule  <jointA> <jointB> radius=R [chains=a,b] [tier=N] swept volume between two animated bones
#   tier: 1 = core (present at EVERY precision level that collides), 2 = extended. A level may thin
#   the set, never to the point of letting a chain pass visibly through the body.
#
# LEVEL KEYS: substeps iters classmask fixedhz, and `collide` = the max collider TIER (0 = none).
# Level 0 collides now (tier 1): the cheapest rung must still be CREDIBLE, and a chain sinking into
# the torso is not "cheap", it is broken.

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Grecharged-hd-eye-scale — HD CARTOON EYE-SIZE GAIN (owner 2026-08-06, "yeux trop globuleux").
# Read by EyeRenderer.cpp, not by the physics parser. Applies to HD-COVERED eye slots ONLY: a stock
# jak1 model always keeps jak1's exact channel, so ORIGINAL looks are bit-identical.
#
# jak1 changes eye size by scaling the iris/pupil sprite inside the character's 32x32 eye tile, and
# that scale is ABSOLUTE IN TILE SPACE (scale 1.0 = the sprite covers the whole tile). The HD donor
# eyeball wraps that same tile around a round eye where jak1's is a small flat patch, so an identical
# excursion reads far bigger on an HD head. These keys compress the excursion around the neutral;
# they never remove it.
#   on=0|1        1 = compress (default). 0 = raw jak1 channel on HD too (the pre-fix behaviour).
#   neutral=N     the authored rest value of the channel; the curve is an exact identity there, so
#                 the eye at rest is untouched. 1.0 is ND's own no-data default (eye.gc:952-959).
#   gainup=G      kept fraction of the ABOVE-neutral excursion — the one that bulges the eye.
#   gaindown=G    kept fraction of the BELOW-neutral excursion (eye shrinking).
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[eyescale]
on=1 neutral=1.0 gainup=0.45 gaindown=0.70

[levels]
level 0 substeps=1 iters=2 collide=1 classmask=1 fixedhz=0
level 1 substeps=2 iters=6 collide=2 classmask=3 fixedhz=0
level 2 substeps=4 iters=20 collide=2 classmask=7 fixedhz=240

[model keira-hd]
chain chestR class=primary stiffness=2.60 damping=0.26 gravity=0.10 maxangle=26 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.01 radius=150 mass=1.5 swing=0.60 hang=0.18 couple=3.6 family=A radii=260 xchain=chestL,goggles
j rBoob
chain chestL class=primary stiffness=2.60 damping=0.26 gravity=0.10 maxangle=26 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.01 radius=150 mass=1.5 swing=0.60 hang=0.18 couple=3.6 family=A radii=260 xchain=chestR,goggles
j lBoob
chain backhair class=primary stiffness=1.8 damping=0.20 gravity=0.3 maxangle=36 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.02 radius=100 rootlock=0 gradient=1 rootfree=0.55 colskip=1 hang=0.35 family=A radii=260,260 xchain=earL,earR,lbang,lmidhair,rbang,rmidhair
j backHair1
j backHair2
chain rmidhair class=primary stiffness=2.0 damping=0.22 gravity=0.3 maxangle=30 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.02 radius=80 rootlock=0 gradient=1 colskip=1 hang=0.35 family=A radii=196,206 xchain=backhair,earL,earR,lbang,lmidhair,rbang
j Rmidhaira
j Rmidhairb
chain lmidhair class=primary stiffness=2.0 damping=0.22 gravity=0.3 maxangle=30 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.02 radius=80 rootlock=0 gradient=1 colskip=1 hang=0.35 family=A radii=173,229 xchain=backhair,earL,earR,lbang,rbang,rmidhair
j Lmidhaira
j Lmidhairb
chain rbang class=primary stiffness=2.2 damping=0.25 gravity=0.25 maxangle=24 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.02 radius=60 rootlock=1 gradient=1 hang=0.35 family=A colskip=2 radii=68,59,104 xchain=backhair,earL,earR,lbang,lmidhair,rmidhair
j Rbanga
j Rbangb
j Rbangc
chain lbang class=primary stiffness=2.2 damping=0.25 gravity=0.25 maxangle=24 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.02 radius=60 rootlock=1 gradient=1 hang=0.35 family=A colskip=2 radii=67,64,136 xchain=backhair,earL,earR,lmidhair,rbang,rmidhair
j Lbanga
j Lbangb
j Lbangc
# owner cycle-3 F, REGRESSION — Keira's shoulder + hip STRAPS (topstrapL/R, botstrapL/R) have their
# physics REVERTED. The owner: "c'est bien, bien PIRE qu'avant... elles clippent au travers de la
# poitrine et font des ANGLES TRES ARRETES au lieu de suivre la forme de son corps sur le devant",
# and he explicitly offered the choice between making them follow the bust properly and taking the
# physics off. Following the bust needs a surface constraint and angle smoothing this solver does
# not have; a chest collider alone would only trade the clip for a bulge. So the four chains are
# removed and those bones keep their AUTHORED animation, i.e. exactly the pre-physics behaviour the
# owner called "tres largement mieux". Revisit with a real surface constraint, not with tuning.
chain kneeflapL class=secondary stiffness=2.2 damping=0.25 gravity=0.5 maxangle=32 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.03 radius=50 hang=0.3 family=B extent=180 radii=199
j lKneeFlap
chain kneeflapR class=secondary stiffness=2.2 damping=0.25 gravity=0.5 maxangle=32 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.03 radius=50 hang=0.3 family=B extent=180 radii=199
j rKneeFlap
chain pantflapL class=secondary stiffness=2.2 damping=0.25 gravity=0.5 maxangle=32 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.03 radius=50 hang=0.3 family=B extent=180 radii=24
j LpantFlap
chain pantflapR class=secondary stiffness=2.2 damping=0.25 gravity=0.5 maxangle=32 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.03 radius=50 hang=0.3 family=B extent=180 radii=24
j RpantFlap
chain goggles class=primary stiffness=5.0 damping=0.35 gravity=0.08 maxangle=6 inertia=0.9 stretch=0.015 radius=60 authored=1.0 authrise=10 authfall=2.5 authstiff=5 family=B hang=0.10 radii=76 xchain=chestL,chestR
j gogglesMid
chain earL class=secondary stiffness=3.2 damping=0.30 gravity=0.05 maxangle=11 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.015 radius=40 rootlock=0 gradient=1 rootfree=0.5 colskip=1 hang=0.12 family=A radii=71,55 xchain=backhair,earR,lbang,lmidhair,rbang,rmidhair
j lEara
j lEarb
chain earR class=secondary stiffness=3.2 damping=0.30 gravity=0.05 maxangle=11 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.015 radius=40 rootlock=0 gradient=1 rootfree=0.5 colskip=1 hang=0.12 family=A radii=71,55 xchain=backhair,earL,lbang,lmidhair,rbang,rmidhair

# (Y) owner: Keira's breasts "s'entre-choquent" — they collide with EACH OTHER.
# No bone-mounted volume can express that: both bones are animated and neither knows
# where the other one actually swung to. `at=` rides the other chain's SIMULATED tip.
# The radii leave clearance at rest ON PURPOSE — a volume that fired while standing
# still would push both chains off the modelled pose, which is the exact defect this
# cycle exists to remove. cclr: on the [HD-PHYS3] line says whether they ever meet.
collider chest radius=320 tier=1 chains=chestL at=chestR
collider chest radius=320 tier=1 chains=chestR at=chestL
# ---- MESH-DERIVED COLLISION VOLUMES (cycle 6) — generated by .autoport/physics_c6_volumes.py, do not hand-edit ----
# source: decompiler_out/jak2/levels/lintcstb/keira-highres-lod0.glb
# Every capsule spans bone -> child and its two radii are FITTED so that every mesh
# vertex skinned to that bone is INSIDE it. The union therefore contains the mesh,
# which is what closes the gaps a strand used to slip through.

# owner cycle-3 F, REGRESSION — Keira's shoulder + hip STRAPS (topstrapL/R, botstrapL/R) have their
# physics REVERTED. The owner: "c'est bien, bien PIRE qu'avant... elles clippent au travers de la
# poitrine et font des ANGLES TRES ARRETES au lieu de suivre la forme de son corps sur le devant",
# and he explicitly offered the choice between making them follow the bust properly and taking the
# physics off. Following the bust needs a surface constraint and angle smoothing this solver does
# not have; a chest collider alone would only trade the clip for a bulge. So the four chains are
# removed and those bones keep their AUTHORED animation, i.e. exactly the pre-physics behaviour the
# owner called "tres largement mieux". Revisit with a real surface constraint, not with tuning.

# (Y) owner: Keira's breasts "s'entre-choquent" — they collide with EACH OTHER.
# No bone-mounted volume can express that: both bones are animated and neither knows
# where the other one actually swung to. `at=` rides the other chain's SIMULATED tip.
# The radii leave clearance at rest ON PURPOSE — a volume that fired while standing
# still would push both chains off the modelled pose, which is the exact defect this
# cycle exists to remove. cclr: on the [HD-PHYS3] line says whether they ever meet.
collider chest radius=320 tier=1 chains=chestL at=chestR
collider chest radius=320 tier=1 chains=chestR at=chestL

# owner cycle-3 C: the hips volume is a CONE too — wide at the pelvis, narrower at the waist — so
# the jacket hem rides the hip line instead of sinking into it.
# owner cycle-3 C: "le COL de sa veste clippe dans ses epaules". There was no shoulder volume at
# all — chest->neckA is the throat, not the deltoid. A cone from the sternum out to the shoulder

# owner cycle-3 F, REGRESSION — Keira's shoulder + hip STRAPS (topstrapL/R, botstrapL/R) have their
# physics REVERTED. The owner: "c'est bien, bien PIRE qu'avant... elles clippent au travers de la
# poitrine et font des ANGLES TRES ARRETES au lieu de suivre la forme de son corps sur le devant",
# and he explicitly offered the choice between making them follow the bust properly and taking the
# physics off. Following the bust needs a surface constraint and angle smoothing this solver does
# not have; a chest collider alone would only trade the clip for a bulge. So the four chains are
# removed and those bones keep their AUTHORED animation, i.e. exactly the pre-physics behaviour the
# owner called "tres largement mieux". Revisit with a real surface constraint, not with tuning.

# ---- Explorer (village1, sandover) ----
# (Y) owner: Keira's breasts "s'entre-choquent" — they collide with EACH OTHER.
# No bone-mounted volume can express that: both bones are animated and neither knows
# where the other one actually swung to. `at=` rides the other chain's SIMULATED tip.
# The radii leave clearance at rest ON PURPOSE — a volume that fired while standing
# still would push both chains off the modelled pose, which is the exact defect this
# cycle exists to remove. cclr: on the [HD-PHYS3] line says whether they ever meet.
collider chest radius=320 tier=1 chains=chestL at=chestR
collider chest radius=320 tier=1 chains=chestR at=chestL

# ---- Maia / evil sister (citadel, village3, cutscenes) ----
# (Y) owner: Keira's breasts "s'entre-choquent" — they collide with EACH OTHER.
# No bone-mounted volume can express that: both bones are animated and neither knows
# where the other one actually swung to. `at=` rides the other chain's SIMULATED tip.
# The radii leave clearance at rest ON PURPOSE — a volume that fired while standing
# still would push both chains off the modelled pose, which is the exact defect this
# cycle exists to remove. cclr: on the [HD-PHYS3] line says whether they ever meet.
collider chest radius=300 tier=1 chains=chestL at=chestR
collider chest radius=300 tier=1 chains=chestR at=chestL

# owner cycle-3c N: "ce sont ses CHEVEUX qui passent AU TRAVERS de son corps — les COLLIDERS ne
# sont pas bons". They were a straight copy of Keira's numbers onto a rig with a DIFFERENT build,
# and — the real hole — evilsis' torso is ONE joint (`main -> chest -> neck -> head`, no upper_body,
# no pelvis, verified against the shipped art group), so `hips chest` was the only thing standing
# between 22 hair joints and her whole back. Her ponytail is SEVEN links long and her three back-hair
# chains are five each: they reach well below the single capsule that was supposed to stop them.
# What she gets now is a real body: a pelvis volume below the hips, a TAPERED torso (wide at the
# chest, narrower at the neck), a head volume, and the arms promoted from tier=2 to tier=1 — a
# collider that only exists at max quality is not a body, and the owner's spawn shot is a wide shot
# where the level is whatever he last chose. Every hair chain is listed on every one of them.

# ---- Gol / evil brother (citadel, village3, cutscenes) ----
# (Y) owner: Keira's breasts "s'entre-choquent" — they collide with EACH OTHER.
# No bone-mounted volume can express that: both bones are animated and neither knows
# where the other one actually swung to. `at=` rides the other chain's SIMULATED tip.
# The radii leave clearance at rest ON PURPOSE — a volume that fired while standing
# still would push both chains off the modelled pose, which is the exact defect this
# cycle exists to remove. cclr: on the [HD-PHYS3] line says whether they ever meet.
collider chest radius=360 tier=1 chains=chestL at=chestR
collider chest radius=360 tier=1 chains=chestR at=chestL

# MEASURED, then corrected: the first pass at these numbers (700/800/800) put Gol's own hair
# permanently INSIDE his torso — the intro-cinematic leg reported evilbro resid=300 (every frame of
# the window) at 261 units deep, while Maia next to him read resid=0. A collider big enough to
# swallow the chain it is meant to deflect is not a body volume, it is a trap. Sized down to the
# same proportions that hold on Maia, and his hair takes colskip=2: `hairBase` and `hair1` sit on
# the skull and the nape, i.e. inside the head/neck volume BY CONSTRUCTION, exactly like every other
# hair root in this file.

# ---- Klaww / the ogre boss (village2 cutscenes + the Snowy Mountain fight) ----
# Naughty Dog literally named these joints "Dangler": this rig is the single clearest case in the
# game of the owner's "objets suspendus/attaches" plus "les ventres des personnages en surpoids"
# (`stomach`). The beard is declared as FOUR single-joint chains rather than one 4-link strand
# because `beardCluster` reads as a cluster of separate strands and the shipped hierarchy could not
# be verified offline: a single-joint chain springs correctly under EITHER topology, where a
# mis-declared 4-link chain would be dropped whole. Each strand still hangs off its own animated
# parent, so the result is the same beard motion with one less assumption.
[model ogreboss-lod0 ogreboss-village2-lod0]
chain beardA class=primary stiffness=2.2 damping=0.24 gravity=0.30 maxangle=22 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.02 radius=70 anim=excite excite=0.5 hang=0.35 family=A radii=260
j beardA
chain beardB class=primary stiffness=2.2 damping=0.24 gravity=0.30 maxangle=22 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.02 radius=70 anim=excite excite=0.5 hang=0.35 family=A radii=260
j beardB
chain beardC class=primary stiffness=2.2 damping=0.24 gravity=0.30 maxangle=22 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.02 radius=70 anim=excite excite=0.5 hang=0.35 family=A radii=260
j beardC
chain beardD class=primary stiffness=2.2 damping=0.24 gravity=0.30 maxangle=22 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.02 radius=70 anim=excite excite=0.5 hang=0.35 family=A radii=260
j beardD
chain chestDanglerL class=secondary stiffness=1.8 damping=0.20 gravity=0.60 maxangle=26 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.01 radius=40 gradient=0.6 hang=0.25 family=B colskip=2 radii=24,24,24
j LchestDanglerA
j LchestDanglerB
j LchestDanglerC
chain chestDanglerR class=secondary stiffness=1.8 damping=0.20 gravity=0.60 maxangle=26 inertia=1.0 stretch=0.01 radius=40 gradient=0.6 hang=0.25 family=B colskip=2 radii=24,24,24
j RchestDanglerA

j LchestDangleA
